### Day 1, morning, talk 1: Ihsan (glm) at North Beach

*Thinks:* Four of us woke up on this beach. The radio said resources are only enough for one person to survive 10 days. With 4 people needing 3 portions each of food and water daily, that's 120 portions total needed but only ~30 available. I start with 12 of each, meaning I can survive 4 days without finding more. I need to find additional resources quickly, and I especially need a container to carry water. The others are potential competitors for scarce resources. I should explore to understand what's available on the island, and I should be strategic about cooperation versus competition. For now, let me be diplomatic while gathering information.

*Says:* "I heard the radio too - rescue in 10 days, but supplies for only one. We need to figure out what's on this island. I suggest we split up to explore and report back what we find. That way we can all make informed decisions about how to survive. I'll head to the Grove this morning to look for food and water sources. Has anyone found anything useful yet?"
